Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the laws framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 616. The resulting operator achieves bound O(308/n^4).

This completes the proof. QED.
